```
20	interface Room { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }
21	
22	function inter(a: Room, b: Room): boolean {
23	  // C# Rectangle.Intersects:仅边接触不算相交
24	  return a.x < b.x + b.w && b.x < a.x + a.w && a.y < b.y + b.h && b.y < a.y + a.h;

27	function setBrick(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
28	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
29	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
30	  st.type[i] = BRICK; st.flags[i] = 1; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
31	  // ★写砖必清坡位（cs:33760-33763 房连线列 / 33786-33788 房实心 / 33563-33566
32	  //   outerTempled / 33499-33503 templeCleaner else / 34161-34165+34181-34185 门框柱,

34	  //   五连写）。神庙壳压在丛林泥/洞穴坡面格上,不清坡 → 幻影坡幸存到 @52
35	  //   （W 批全图残余 351 格根源;与 TileRunner ClearSlope 同族写入语义）。
36	  st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;
37	}

39	/** SolidTile(i, j)（cs:70155）：active + tileSolid + !tileSolidTop + 非半砖 + slope 0。
40	 *  与 SolidTile2（cs:70178，祭坛锚点门用）差一个 tileSolidTop（平台）条件,勿合并。 */
41	function solidTileST(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
42	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
43	  const j = st.idx(x, y);
44	  if (!st.flags[j]) return false;
45	  const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[j]];
46	  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[j] && st.slope[j] === 0;
47	}

53	  const { w, h } = st;
```

```
327	  //   ProtectionType.None。即原版 Legacy 地牢对走廊/挖空 **无任何房间保护**（TilesAndWalls
328	  //   分支在 Legacy 房型上不可达——覆写至多返 Walls）。本仓 prot() 返 Walls（外框内）是
329	  //   "同值重刷跳过"级近似：下游三循环仅用它跳过 chWall(chWall 写的就是同一 wall 值)，

331	  function prot(x: number, y: number, list: DRoom[]): number {
332	    let p = 0;
333	    for (const rm of list) {
334	      const q: number = rm.outer.contains(x, y) ? 2 : 0;
335	      if (q === 1) p = p === 2 ? 3 : 1;
336	      else if (q === 2) p = p === 1 ? 3 : 2;
337	      if (p === 3) break;
338	    }
339	    return p;
340	  }

344	    return rooms.filter((rm) =>
```

```
	public void Clear(TileDataType types)
	{
		if ((types & TileDataType.Tile) != 0)
		{
			type = 0;
			active(active: false);
			frameX = 0;
			frameY = 0;
		}
		if ((types & TileDataType.Wall) != 0)
		{
			wall = 0;
			wallFrameX(0);
			wallFrameY(0);

		if ((types & TileDataType.TilePaint) != 0)

			ClearBlockPaintAndCoating();
		}
		if ((types & TileDataType.WallPaint) != 0)
		{
			ClearWallPaintAndCoating();
		}
		if ((types & TileDataType.Liquid) != 0)
		{
			liquid = 0;
			liquidType(0);
			checkingLiquid(checkingLiquid: false);
		}
		if ((types & TileDataType.Slope) != 0)
		{
			slope(0);
			halfBrick(halfBrick: false);

			wire4(wire4: false);

			actuator(actuator: false);
			inActive(inActive: false);
		}
	}
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-17T00:58:38.177Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Confirmed: the entire dungeon brick write family in vanilla goes through `DungeonUtils.ChangeTileType` which ALWAYS does `tile.Clear(TileDataType.Slope)` = slope=0 + halfBrick=false, regardless of resetTile. And `ChangeWallType` with resetTile=true does ClearEverything (which also zeroes slope/half via sTileHeader=0) — but then only writes wall, leaving tile inactive. So JS `chWall(i, wl, true)` reset branch must also clear slope/half (ClearEverything semantics).

Wait — careful: does the truth-side (x86) dump show slope of inactive tiles? The slope channel dump (`WriteGrid` SW_HS_DUMP) probably dumps the slope byte for all tiles regardless of active. The @52 comparison counts slope/half diffs across the whole map — including inactive cells? The Y batch says "@22 slope/half=0 差" — at pass 22, no diffs anywhere. And @52 219 residuals are in the dungeon region. Since ChangeWallType(reset=true) via ClearEverything would clear slope on those interior cells in truth, JS chWall reset branch without slope clear would leave phantom slope on now-inactive cells — these could be part of the 219.

So the fix for chTile:
```ts
function chTile(i: number, t: number, reset: boolean): void {
    if (reset) {
      st.type[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 0; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
      st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;   // ClearEverything (Tile.cs:140-151) 也清坡位
    }
    st.flags[i] = 1; st.type[i] = t;
    st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;     // ChangeTileType 无条件 Clear(Slope)（DU:1611-1612）
}
```

And chWall:
```ts
function chWall(i: number, wl: number, reset: boolean): void {
    if (reset) {
      st.type[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 0; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
      st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;   // ClearEverything（无后续 Clear(Slope)——ChangeWallType 不清坡）
    }
    st.wall[i] = wl;
}
```

Now, does TileStore have `slope` and `half` arrays? TemplePass uses `st.slope[i]` and `st.half[i]` — yes.

Now, other write points that don't go through chTile:
1. Lines 1308-1324: SPIKE writes — vanilla: `DungeonUtils.ChangeTileType(Main.tile[num21, num23], 48, resetTile: false)` (DungeonPitTrap.cs:296/300)! So spikes also go through ChangeTileType → clear slope. Line 1266 `chTile(ti, SPIKE, false)` already goes through chTile. But lines 1308-1324 are separate direct writes. Let me read that context.

2. Line 1522: DOOR write — vanilla PlaceTile(10). WorldGen.PlaceTile — need to check if it clears slope.

3. Line 1951: `st.flags[ti] = 1; st.type[ti] = tile;` — what's this? Read context.

4. Line 2081: DSWITCH — WorldGen.PlaceTile(135)?

5. Line 2387: `st.flags[i] = 1; st.type[i] = t;` — the Place* family. Vanilla WorldGen.PlaceTile.

6. setTileSilent (2862, 2909) — furniture placement. Vanilla WorldGen.PlaceTile / place via ObjectPlacement.

7. tileRunner — already handled.

8. Line 946: `st.wall[ti] = 0; chTile(ti, brick, false)` — fine with chTile fix.

Now: does WorldGen.PlaceTile clear slope in vanilla? Let me check WorldGen.PlaceTile. In vanilla 1.4, WorldGen.PlaceTile near the end: `tile.active(active: true); tile.type = type;` plus maybe `tile.halfBrick(false)`? Let me look.
````

</details>

```
So PlaceTile's generic write (default case) is `tile.active(true); tile.type = num;` — no slope clear when the tile was already active. Slope only cleared when tile was NOT active (via `tile.Clear(...Slope)`) or for specific types.

So:
- For furniture/decoration placement on EMPTY tiles (typical dungeon furniture on floors/walls): slope cleared (tile was inactive).
- For placement on ACTIVE tiles: slope kept.

But wait — in the dungeon, furniture is placed on empty interior cells (after hollowing), so those were inactive → cleared slope anyway. The phantom-slope issue is mainly about bricks written by ChangeTileType over sloped dirt/stone/eblestone cells (the room/hall/entrance/paths carve-and-fill). Those are the 219.

C. Writes mirroring WorldGen.PlaceTile (DOOR 1522, DSWITCH 2081, placeTile 1951, Place* family 2387, setTileSilent 2862/2909, pressure plates 2208/2210): vanilla clears slope only when tile inactive. - `ChangeWallType` = (optional ClearEverything) + wall.
```
```
- `PlaceTile` = if !active → Clear(Tile|TilePaint|Slope); default write active(true)+type.
```

```
	private Point Lights_GenerateSwitch(int x, int y)
	{
		Point result = Point.Zero;
		for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
		{
			int num = x + WorldGen.genRand.Next(-12, 13);
			int num2 = y + WorldGen.genRand.Next(3, 21);
			if (Main.tile[num, num2].active() || Main.tile[num, num2 + 1].active() || !DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type) || !DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type) || !Collision.CanHit(new Point(num * 16, num2 * 16), 16, 16, new Point(x * 16, y * 16 + 1), 16, 16))
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (((WorldGen.SolidTile(num - 1, num2) && Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type != 10) || (WorldGen.SolidTile(num + 1, num2) && Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type != 10) || WorldGen.SolidTile(num, num2 + 1)) && DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[num, num2].wall) && (DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type) || DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type)))
			{
				WorldGen.PlaceTile(num, num2, 136, mute: true);
				result = new Point(num, num2);
			}
			if (!Main.tile[num, num2].active())
			{
				continue;
			}
			while (num != x || num2 != y)
			{
				Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
				if (num > x)
				{
					num--;
				}
				if (num < x)
				{
					num++;
				}
				Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
				if (num2 > y)
				{
					num2--;
				}
				if (num2 < y)
				{
					num2++;
				}
				Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
			}
			if (WorldGen.genRand.Next(3) > 0)
			{
				Main.tile[x, y].frameX = 18;
				Main.tile[x, y + 1].frameX = 18;
			}
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
```

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-17T01:33:14.812Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
// 一次性诊断（dungeonI 转写债，2026-08-16 已修复收档）：JS↔oracle 网格逐格 diff。
// 输入：/tmp/cs-{dgg,dgi}.{typ,act}（oracle GRID_DUMP=dungeon{G,I}:/tmp/cs-dg{g,i}）与
// /tmp/js-{dgg,dgi}.{typ,act}（_dgidump.test.ts 产物，typ=internal id → 此处经
// TILE_DEFS.vanilla.sheet 映射回原版空间）。缺文件时跳过（默认不进全量跑）。
import { it } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';

const W = 4200, H = 1200;
const READY = existsSync('/tmp/cs-dgi.typ') && existsSync('/tmp/js-dgi.typ');

function loadTyp(p: string, mapped: boolean): Int32Array {
  const buf = readFileSync(p);
  const n = W * H;
  const out = new Int32Array(n);
  let vid: Int32Array | null = null;
  if (mapped) {
    vid = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
    for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
      const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
      if (typeof s === 'number') vid[i] = s;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const v = buf.readUInt16LE(i * 2);
    out[i] = mapped ? (vid ? vid[v] : v) : v;
  }
  return out;
}

```

```
function diff(tag: string): void {
  const csT = loadTyp(`/tmp/cs-${tag}.typ`, false);
  const jsT = loadTyp(`/tmp/js-${tag}.typ`, true);
  const csA = readFileSync(`/tmp/cs-${tag}.act`);
  const jsA = readFileSync(`/tmp/js-${tag}.act`);
  let actDiff = 0, typDiff = 0, ghoDiff = 0;
  const cells: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const ca = csA[i] !== 0, ja = jsA[i] !== 0;
    if (ca !== ja) {
      actDiff++;
      if (cells.length < 40) cells.push(`ACT x=${i % W} y=${(i / W) | 0} cs=${ca ? 1 : 0}/${csT[i]} js=${ja ? 1 : 0}/${jsT[i]}`);
      continue;
    }
    if (ca && csT[i] !== jsT[i]) {
      typDiff++;
      if (cells.length < 40) cells.push(`TYP x=${i % W} y=${(i / W) | 0} cs=${csT[i]} js=${jsT[i]}`);
    } else if (!ca && csT[i] !== jsT[i]) ghoDiff++;
  }
  console.log(`[${tag}] actDiff=${actDiff} typDiff(活性)=${typDiff} ghostDiff=${ghoDiff}`);
  for (const c of cells) console.log('  ' + c);
}

(READY ? it : it.skip)('diff dungeonG/dungeonI grids', () => {
  if (existsSync('/tmp/cs-dgg.typ')) diff('dgg');
  diff('dgi');
});

```

```
  return false;

/**
 * 灯具→墙上开关 + 红线布线(cs L18942-18975/18990-19023):
 * 在灯具 ±12/+3..20 内找两侧皆砖的嵌墙位放开关(136),沿曼哈顿路径铺红线。
 * chandelier=true 时开关位禁邻尖刺(原版两分支条件差异)。
function canHit(st: TileStore, x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number): boolean {
  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
  let num = x1, num2 = y1;
  const num3 = Math.max(1, Math.min(w - 1, x2));
  const num4 = Math.max(1, Math.min(h - 40, y2));
  num = Math.max(1, Math.min(w - 1, num));
  num2 = Math.max(1, Math.min(h - 40, num2));
  const fullBrick = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const k = st.idx(x, y);
    if (!st.flags[k]) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]];
    return !!d?.solid && !d.platform && st.slope[k] === 0 && !st.half[k];
  };

```

```
**①清坡收口（11 修点，全带 1456 行号锚）** — `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/vanilla/DungeonPass.ts`（独占文件，AA 批修件零回退）：
- 根因：原版地牢全部砖写经 `DungeonUtils.ChangeTileType`（DU:1605-1618）= 可选 ClearEverything + active(true) + **无条件 `Clear(TileDataType.Slope)`**；入口城堡/垛口/顶齿/阶梯/门柱直写族同带 `Clear(Slope)` 尾缀。JS 只写 flags/type → 坡位幸存。
- 修点：`chTile`（一处收口覆盖 30+ 调用点）+ `chWall` reset 支 + 厅/入口厅内挖（ClearTile 语义）+ 门侧槽×4（ClearEverything）+ spikeRun 新格×4 + `dgWr`（Place* 全家）+ `placeSimple` + `dgSwitch` + 门锚格（PlaceTile 前奏仅锚格仅 inactive；j2±1 保坡=原版行为）+ `placeFurn`/`placeWallArt` 写入环。
```
